You have taken the position that the five sicknesses, and the five elements, and the five demons are all the direct result of the five poisons--the five bad thoughts. But when the non-Buddhists take the position that phlegm and desire have a cause-and-effect relationship, and that bile and anger have the same kind of relationship, and so on, then Master Dharmakirti refutes them by showing that desire doesn't always come and go according to the phlegm, and anger doesn't always come and go according to the bile. To do so he makes the statement that says, \"It's not a fact that wind and the rest are such, for the relationship doesn't always hold.\" We could twist around this statement too and say that it disproved your position; and add as well how wrong it is to assert that uncreated space could ever be the direct result of jealousy. [The point seems to be that, although your position about the bad thoughts, and our original position on the nature of the three beings' perceptions, are both correct, you could always twist around some quotation by a master, take it out of context or misinterpret it, and try to show they were wrong.]"

What he's talking about here is a belief of the [non- Buddhist] Numerist School. They give the case of a single person whose physical form is looked upon at the same time by his enemy, and also by his friend. In reality, the person's physical form is both attractive and ugly at the same time. Something happens where yet another physical form, one from karma, grows up between the person's true physical form and the enemy and friend looking at it. Because of this neither the enemy nor the friend sees both the attractiveness and the ugliness together. Master Dharmakirti uses logic to refute this concept, and this same logic can be used against the position you have taken, to prove that you are wrong."

    "english": "This is a fact, for the actual meaning of Master Dharmakirti's statement is as follows. The Numerist School is describing a situation where you are looking at a physical form either from far away, or from up close. They say that, depending on the distance between you and the object, another physical form which is the result of karma, and which stands between your visual consciousness and the original form, is either clear or not. This then determines whether the original form appears to you distinctly or not. It is not the case though, they say, that what determines whether the original form appears clearly or not is whether or not you have a clear impression of this form. In reply then Master Dharmakirti is asking the Numerists: Let's consider these two cases of some intermediate physical form that comes from karma. Do they, or do they not, function to obscure the two original forms, the one at a distance, and the other close by? If they were to obscure them, then your visual consciousness could never see the two original forms, since they would have been obscured by the others."

When an earlier instance of the perception of blue ends, what actually happens is that it plants a mental seed which eventually grows into a later instance of the same perception of blue, when the seed ripens. Suppose the blue were not just this kind of appearance, but rather an appearance where blue as an outer object were transmitting a likeness of itself and thereby appearing to one's perceptions. Something else then would be happening when the three different beings sit down together and look at the glass full of something wet and flowing. The three different objects would be appearing to them because each of the objects was transmitting a likeness of itself to their perception. ===== root-review-targets.md =====
# Withheld root review leads — C05:280–282 preparation only

Original source/context and full raw spine records are retained. All six
ACIP/English fields occur once in the unchanged physical C05 original under
ASCII-whitespace-only lookup. No baseline, original reader, proposal or
accepted data exists. Keep these leads withheld from both original angles.

The53 lexical queries preserve48 full returned entry occurrences; complete
HGM gloss/tier/variant/provenance fields were read. All62 lexical/parallel
queries were reopened with exact full raw-row equality. Empty exact compounds
and NULL glosses do not prove no source correspondence. C15:426/427 match
281/282 in all3text fields. C15:425 matches280 Tibetan and differs in English
only by adding the19-character Translator's note: prefix just inside the
opening bracket. Removing that exact prefix for comparison produces the whole
1234-character C05 English from the1253-character witness; no text was edited.
Digital identity does not prove independent publication or ingestion lineage.

280: The long final bracketed paragraph is already translator apparatus,
without separately corresponding Tibetan. Do not align its repeated position,
karma or master's words. Earlier explanatory paraphrases and the short quoted
verse have distinct source owners.4621 chos kyi grags pa provides Dharmakirti/
Master Dharmakirti;11177 dpal glorious. Count the source name independently
from the second English Master Dharmakirti and later apparatus a master.

280: Separate nad/sicknesses10147, 'byung ba/elements13375, gdon/demons9048,
dug lnga/five poisons23396 and nyon mongs pa/bad thoughts5900. GMR restates
five before bad thoughts; do not invent another Tibetan numeral.3743 dngos
'bras explicitly supports direct result, twice in different source relations.
12315 bad kan phlegm,1475/1477 mkhris/mkhris pa bile,9635 'dod chags desire
are positive glossary fields.16268 zhe sdang has only auto-aligned dislike,
so any anger attestation rests on current source wording, not a invented
curated anger variant.2916 rgyu 'bras is curated cause and effect; the English
cause-and-effect relationship and later same kind of relationship require
actual source/phrase boundaries, not normalized quote words.

280:22934 rjes su 'gro ldog attests come and go, while1604 'khrul includes
doesn't always/doesn't/not a fact. Each of the three 'khrul instances has its
own English clause; the verse's ma yin is a separate negation.2592 'gog byed
supports refutes,3104 sgyur ba twist around,10388 gnod pa disproved.16482
gzhung is noisy auto-aligned three principal paths, not proof of this
statement's title;9709 'don pa has reciting/reading/twist around among full
variants. Use the whole statement's source relation before choosing words.
9597 'dus ma byas has uncreated,10167 nam mkha' space,12087 phrag dog jealousy,
24609 mi rigs pa how wrong it is/wrong among variants; do not make an isolated
negative claim from the whole idiom.16393 gzhan yang is another noisy
auto-aligned entry; retain the actual opening relation as provisional evidence.

281: Embedded verse contains bracketed [these different beings] supplied in
English and lineation capitals; neither authorizes reconstructed Tibetan.
17446 yid explicitly has perceptions,7220 tha dad differing,3646 dngos thing,
4216 gcig single. Contrast the one object of the verse with the opponent's
three objects and three ways; repeated numeral/predicate instances are not
interchangeable.11049 snang tshul includes appearing and ways of appearing;
14183 mi 'dra ba different is a positive whole idiom, not negative-prefix
alignment.24799 tshad grub pa has confirmed by a valid perception; examine
whole phrase depth and possible tighter constituents instead of assuming a
dictionary field alone makes any span d5.

282: First source yan lag mi 'dra ba lngas is paraphrased as all four of his
limbs, and his head. The English four plus head expresses five components;
never pair lngas with four or call that contrast a numeral erratum without
further proof. The later yan lag is protuberances of his body, with17346
yan lag positively supporting protuberances and limbs among all variants.
35336 lngas has NULL gloss;3997 lnga has five. All repeated action/five/
performing occurrences, negated one-person conclusion and final reason need
exact canonical ranges.12506 bya ba has actions,12732 byas pa performing/
perform among variants.17005 'o na includes well now/according to you then,
but the source's consequence scope and supplied English participants still
control any retained phrase. No new erratum is proposed by this preparation.

===== tibetan/280/report.md =====
C05:280 — Codex, independent Tibetan-first original proposal; PROVISIONAL machine alignment, unreviewed.
The final 'khrul/doesn't always hold is the first challenge; relationship stays supplied. The second rgyu 'bras, chos and 'don pa are omitted with reasons.
HGM HTG2016 supports phlegm, come and go, direct result, uncreated and jealousy. The exact zhe sdang entry only has provisional dislike; anger is independently attested in the original passage.
Full source, complete SQL results and HGM entries: ../280/evidence/index.json; queries SHA-256 39c13f56b5ca5fa33578aa52082010b277841403bfb8acb20cd3411e5ba35be4.
All six snapshot fields match full original SQLite rows. Original C05 ASCII columns match after whitespace joining; exact line ranges and text are in ../280/evidence/physical-row-comparisons.json.
C15/P7 textual comparisons and limits are recorded in ../280/evidence/parallel-comparisons.json and queries.json; no publication or ingestion independence asserted. Errata: 0; registered classes checked.
Counts: 50 spans; 0 nulls; 48 d5 (48 nonnull); 1 d6; 1 d3; 0 d7. All intended ranges and complete rendered source columns verified.
Final generator: EXIT=0; BODY_BYTES=8093; STDERR_BYTES=0. Exact streams/input/exit: runs/001/. Real generator uses ../280/run_generator_ro.py solely to force SQLite URI mode=ro.
Spec SHA-256: c6252dd25a7c69b11bd16d36dd09b30d91e1f65a4b72f1bd7027f7deb68f1a22; body SHA-256: 1050e4343fbb96cc3da0b10944ea5952ae22bd9648020dd9c107956a034fe9cc.
intended-spans.json declares every exact occurrence in spec order. decisions.json preserves omissions/contentions; history and ../280/evidence/attempts preserve superseded hypotheses and failures.
Files are frozen by freeze.json; mechanical success does not establish semantic correspondence or human approval.

===== tibetan/281/report.md =====
C05:281 — Codex, independent Tibetan-first original proposal; PROVISIONAL machine alignment, unreviewed.
snang/appearing and tshul/ways preserve both components of the discontinuous rendering. na re/claim is supported by the exact HGM entry; mi 'dra ba owns only the three modifiers of ways.
Exact HTG2016 entries support snang/appearing, tshul/ways, na re/claim and tshad grub pa/confirmed by a valid perception. don ma grub par is a complete d3 recast.
Full source, complete SQL results and HGM entries: ../280/evidence/index.json; queries SHA-256 39c13f56b5ca5fa33578aa52082010b277841403bfb8acb20cd3411e5ba35be4.
All six snapshot fields match full original SQLite rows. Original C05 ASCII columns match after whitespace joining; exact line ranges and text are in ../280/evidence/physical-row-comparisons.json.
C15/P7 textual comparisons and limits are recorded in ../280/evidence/parallel-comparisons.json and queries.json; no publication or ingestion independence asserted. Errata: 0; registered classes checked.
Counts: 44 spans; 1 nulls; 37 d5 (37 nonnull); 6 d6; 1 d3; 0 d7. All intended ranges and complete rendered source columns verified.
Final generator: EXIT=0; BODY_BYTES=6833; STDERR_BYTES=0. Exact streams/input/exit: runs/001/. Real generator uses ../280/run_generator_ro.py solely to force SQLite URI mode=ro.
Spec SHA-256: 90a9611167067af12a3542cc2bb3fbf9b5b37addca29bbb1bc2b8c958f6eca9a; body SHA-256: b340a676d71e541bdc89fda0fada2119e329654b8b62302207c11a8d14903c12.

Files are frozen by freeze.json; mechanical success does not establish semantic correspondence or human approval.

Challenge first: don ma grub par → has no reality is a complete phrase recast; the three appearance predicates remain depth 3 to avoid assigning distributed appearing/ways to separate lexemes.
Omitted expanded report frames and inferential packaging. Bracketed beings stays unwrapped; prose 'gro ba owns the later beings. Zhing owns and between wet/flowing.
Complete original rows, physical ASCII source, six-field equality, glossary/corpus query results and hashes: evidence-references.json; segment judgments: decisions.json.

===== reconciled/281/report.md =====
C05:281 — PROVISIONAL reconciliation by Codex, a non-author of either current original. Retained campaign and prior tooling context is disclosed; this is model inspection, not human certification or semantic approval.

The final candidate contains 46 spans, 2 justified grammatical nulls, and 36 nonnull d5 selections. All 76 original identities have individual retained/recast dispositions with complete original span objects, key order, optional-field presence, parent and intended-range declarations. Every original phrase remainder and material unselected expression is explained in omissions.json; full unwrapped character complements are retained.

The three complete appearance-phrase originals are recast to separate snang/appearing, tshul/ways, mi 'dra ba/different and gsum/three selections. This preserves secure useful lexical coverage without losing the manner noun. Their arising/existence frame is distributed, not falsely nulled. Tshad grub pa is retained as the established valid-cognition-confirmed technical compound at d5; the decision rests on its actual technical usage as well as the complete HTG2016 entry, not simply dictionary inclusion. Na re/claim is retained at grammatical d6; kha cig/Someone and yang/yet another keep narrow ownership. Ni and gyis are justified grammatical nulls; la/at is retained at the correct glass-object occurrence. Broader inference/reporting and bracketed participant frames remain unwrapped.

All six original canonical inputs were replayed once here and reproduced their frozen bodies. This final segment was generated once by the unchanged canonical generator through the saved URI read-only/query_only wrapper: actual EXIT 0, empty stderr, exact saved stdout/body and all declared ranges verified. Full generated source text equals both original spine fields after removing markup/null annotations. No note/spec changes followed that execution.

Final spec: 6989 bytes, SHA256 dd2509c4e938bf4b0080f2effbdcc0740b464eec94501dc1222a6d6d0508573f. Final body: 7129 bytes, SHA256 bb020d123737f2bf4cf9772dc3449a457728a33adca4e3eb8a4a1379f6585103. Actual PAT hits: 0. Errata: none; all 3 investigated suspicions have five-ground decisions and are refuted.

Shared complete evidence is under ../280/: original-copy-manifest.json (412 original records), original-tree-preservation.json (all six full original trees and failures), source-aliases.json, fixed-git-sources.json, baseline-fixed-git-bindings.json, query-replays.json (226 actual full SELECT sets with original descriptor/key mapping), archive-object-proof.json (127 master entries and 257 corpus objects), source/context-rows-full.json, physical-proof.json and additional-physical-proof.json (16 complete C05/C15/C16 physical extents), and original-replays/. Complete raw archives and physical originals are content-preserved once with all aliases. P7/ILL corpus evidence establishes only its actual digital extent; digital agreement does not prove independent publication. No dictionary gloss or source spelling has been altered.

Failure history: source-only collection/preflight assumptions were corrected in new helper versions, preserving exact old scripts and streams. They concern the baseline key, empty-list-to-NULL SQL import behavior, wrapper whitespace literal, and relative copy paths. None was an actual canonical failure or a source change. All genuine original failed and superseded proposal versions/canonical attempts remain mapped intact. Detailed role/history and supported-copy preflight are retained under ../280/. This freezes a reconciliation candidate; a distinct semantic reviewer and root acceptance remain required for landing.

===== reconciled/282/report.md =====
C05:282 — PROVISIONAL reconciliation by Codex, a non-author of either current original. Retained campaign and prior tooling context is disclosed; this is model inspection, not human certification or semantic approval.

The final candidate contains 24 spans, 2 justified grammatical nulls, and 19 nonnull d5 selections. All 48 original identities have individual retained/recast dispositions with complete original span objects, key order, optional-field presence, parent and intended-range declarations. Every original phrase remainder and material unselected expression is explained in omissions.json; full unwrapped character complements are retained.

The complete 'o na/Well now transition stays at phrase d3. The negative is narrowed from the full consequence to ma/n't at d6, preserving explicit subword intent. The modal/copula and supplied According to you remain outside. Both gis nulls describe genuine active-subject grammatical fusion. Four limbs plus a head distributes the first five-component statement; it is neither a numeral erratum nor a null. The later lngas/five stays whole, with no manufactured split -s. Every repeated action/difference/numeral owns its declared occurrence.

All six original canonical inputs were replayed once here and reproduced their frozen bodies. This final segment was generated once by the unchanged canonical generator through the saved URI read-only/query_only wrapper: actual EXIT 0, empty stderr, exact saved stdout/body and all declared ranges verified. Full generated source text equals both original spine fields after removing markup/null annotations. No note/spec changes followed that execution.

Final spec: 4126 bytes, SHA256 2b270696d848649127e538e6ce18528ee3fa89ee50d7e2694d58781fc01fcb47. Final body: 4138 bytes, SHA256 61f84b33c667435679b9ae21dae25fe0c8ebe59add59119a26e86df5cfaf4c85. Actual PAT hits: 0. Errata: none; all 3 investigated suspicions have five-ground decisions and are refuted.

Shared complete evidence is under ../280/: original-copy-manifest.json (412 original records), original-tree-preservation.json (all six full original trees and failures), source-aliases.json, fixed-git-sources.json, baseline-fixed-git-bindings.json, query-replays.json (226 actual full SELECT sets with original descriptor/key mapping), archive-object-proof.json (127 master entries and 257 corpus objects), source/context-rows-full.json, physical-proof.json and additional-physical-proof.json (16 complete C05/C15/C16 physical extents), and original-replays/. Complete raw archives and physical originals are content-preserved once with all aliases. P7/ILL corpus evidence establishes only its actual digital extent; digital agreement does not prove independent publication. No dictionary gloss or source spelling has been altered.
